## 2026-07-06 — V61c UPDATE (in place): battle-intent bypass — keep-3 destiny buffer only on turns a battle is plausible
- MOD `server/.../ai/models/rando/evaluators/DecisionContext.java` (~line 309) — ADD `isBattlePlausibleThisTurn()`: the ONE shared battle-intent predicate. Conservative v1 per Steve: battle is "plausible" if ANY location is CONTESTED (both sides' total power > 0 there), the same scan V61b uses in BattleEvaluator (~630). Null game/gameState/playerId/opponent or any exception → true (keep 3). Zero contested locations → false (deploy-and-end turn).
- MOD `server/.../ai/models/rando/evaluators/ForceActivationEvaluator.java` `calculateActivationAmount` (~line 193) — the V61c keep-3 cap (`min(maxAvailable, reserveDeck - 3)`) now applies ONLY when the predicate is true; otherwise activate `maxAvailable` in full. Old always-on line kept commented above. V67at end-game clamp still runs after the branch, unchanged, and still wins when more conservative.
- MOD `server/.../ai/models/rando/evaluators/ActionTextEvaluator.java` V168 carve-out (~line 185) + V38.3 confirm carve-out (~line 1404) — both `reserve <= 3` conditions become `reserve <= 3 && isBattlePlausibleThisTurn()`. No-contested turns fall through to normal V168 ALWAYS ACTIVATE (+5000) and normal V38.3 bounce-back (No +9999). Old conditions kept commented. The second V38.3 arm (`evaluateActivateForce` ~5060, flat +500, no carve-out) untouched — dominated either way.
- Why: Steve (2026-07-01): "If Rando intends to battle that turn, he needs to save 3. If he intends to deploy and end turn without battling he can activate all force." The 2026-06-29 V61c protected the buffer EVERY turn, throttling activation on turns with no possible battle. Activate phase precedes Deploy/Battle, so intent is PREDICTED: contested location exists → battle plausible → keep 3 (old behavior); zero contested → activate all. Bias toward keeping 3 when unsure — a false "no battle" re-opens the no-destiny bug; a false "battle" only costs a little activation.
- Boundary: no new score magnitudes — the change only selects between the two pre-existing regimes (V61c buffer vs V168/V38.3/V57 full-activate). All three sites share the SAME predicate (the original V61c bug was these sites disagreeing), and board presence can't change during the Activate phase, so they cannot split within a turn. V61b (overpower exception) and V67at (end-game reserve-2) untouched. Known blind spot (accepted, v1): Rando can CREATE a contested site by deploying onto opponent presence after activating full; the BattleEvaluator V61 reserve guard (-800/-400/-200 at reserve 0/1/2) still discourages destiny-dependent battles on such turns.
- Verified: marker line `V61c BATTLE-INTENT: no contested location — activating full` logs at each site when the bypass actually changes the outcome (FAE: only when the cap would have clamped; V38.3: once, on the "No" option). Grep-confirmed zero `if (false` in the edited branches (live code) and zero V61c/DESTINY BUFFER logic in chosenone/ (nothing to mirror — chosenone never got V61c). Compile + live-jar byte-check pending with the orchestrator's build.
- Revert: in all three files, delete the `V61c UPDATED 2026-07-06` blocks and un-comment the `V61c pre-2026-07-06 (always-on buffer)` lines directly above each replacement; remove `isBattlePlausibleThisTurn()` from DecisionContext.

## 2026-07-06 — V67bc UPDATE + V191: DPS non-bucket epilogue (into-hand pulls pickable) + top-N candidate logging

- MOD src/.../ai/models/rando/evaluators/CombinedEvaluator.java — V67bc updated in place: EPILOGUE before the DPS-walk PASS return (+ NON_BUCKET_EPILOGUE_FLOOR=+50 constant); V191 top-5 candidate log at the decision site.
- MOD src/.../ai/models/chosenone/evaluators/CombinedEvaluator.java — exact mirror of both.
- MOD src/.../ai/models/rando/RandoCalAi.java — V191 breadcrumb on the fallback-heuristic branch (path + final pick; top-5 lives in HeuristicAiBase's private pick loop, not instrumented).
- MOD src/.../ai/models/chosenone/TheChosenOneAi.java — mirror of the breadcrumb.

Why: (audit deploy-sequencing-4) DeployPhaseScript deliberately excludes "take X into hand / into pile" Reserve pulls from every bucket (they don't put a card on table), but the V67bc walk could only ever return FROM a bucket — when all buckets were exhausted it returned PASS, so a well-scored into-hand pull (V116 +100 baseline and up) was unpickable forever. The epilogue picks the best NON-bucket, non-PASS-typed action when it clears +50; otherwise the original PASS runs. V191 logs one line per CombinedEvaluator decision ('V191 TOPN: <decisionType> phase=<phase> :: actionId=score x5', sorted on a COPY so live tie-breaking never moves) — the dominance-regression detector for future rule additions.

Boundary: epilogue is only reachable after every bucket's best is < -100, so it can only replace PASS, never a bucket winner (a +900 non-bucket pull still loses to a +420 STEP 3 deploy). Junk below +50 (e.g. keyword-only ~+45) still passes; -60 non-bucket still passes. PassEvaluator's Cancel-id pass (V27/V27.1 bonuses can stack past +50) is excluded by ActionType. V191 is instrumentation only, zero scoring changes.

Verified: no 'if (false' guard in either CombinedEvaluator (walk is live); rando/chosenone mirrors diff-identical except package/import; brace/paren balance clean; compile deferred to orchestrator.

Revert: delete the V67bc EPILOGUE block + NON_BUCKET_EPILOGUE_FLOOR constant and the V191 blocks in the four files (all-additive change, zero lines removed — plain deletion of the tagged blocks restores prior behavior exactly).

## 2026-07-07 — V177/V82.1 parser fix: objective pull-targets anchored on the pull VERB (Endor Operations + recurring class)
- MOD `rando/strategy/DeckOracle.java` + chosenone mirror, `parseSourceCardPullTargets` (~1163): the "from Reserve Deck" capture regex changed from `([^.;]+?)\s+from\s+reserve\s+deck` (grabs the WHOLE clause back to the previous period) to `[^.;]*\b(take|deploy|download|upload|reveal|retrieve|use|search for|add|put|choose)\b\s+([^.;]*?)\s+from\s+reserve\s+deck` — capture only the object AFTER the last pull-verb before "from Reserve Deck". Also added a quantifier strip (`^(one|two|three|four|up to N)`) to both normalization loops.
- Why (Steve, live game): Rando on Endor Operations (Card8_167) never fired its "may take one Ominous Rumors or Establish Secret Base into hand from Reserve Deck" pull. The old regex captured "While this side up, once during each of your control phases, may take one Ominous Rumors or Establish Secret Base into hand" and comma-split it into garbage targets [while this side up, phases, one ominous rumors, establish secret base]; "while this side up" passed the stopword filter, V177 couldn't find it in Reserve, declared DEAD and hard-blocked (-2000) EVERY turn. This is the recurring "Rando won't pull with his objective" class — any objective whose pull clause has leading timing text (While this side up / once during your X phase) was vulnerable.
- Boundary (regression-tested BEFORE deploy, python harness on the exact regex): Endor Operations OLD [while this side up, phases, ominous rumors, establish secret base] → NEW [ominous rumors, establish secret base]. Preserved unchanged: Invasion "deploy a Naboo site from Reserve Deck" → [naboo site] (the greedy [^.;]* anchors on the LAST "deploy", fixing the original V82.1 concern), "take one capital starship or Effect" → [capital starship, effect], "deploy a battleground site" → [battleground site].
- Verified: compiles clean both bots (MVN_EXIT=0). PENDING: a live Endor Operations game — expect NO "V177 DEAD SEARCH blocked ... source 'Endor Operations'", and Rando taking Ominous Rumors / Establish Secret Base into hand during its control phase.
- Revert: restore the `([^.;]+?)\s+from\s+reserve\s+deck` pattern and remove the quantifier strip in both bots' DeckOracle.

Self-limiting one-shot: once one body lands on the empty site Rando controls it → the not-controlled guard closes → the bonus never stacks per-body, and the rest of the pile reverts to normal siting.
- Boundary math (from the diagnosed game): Landing Platform first body ≈ 660 (empty-bay +80, V136 CS 350→600, drain +30, high-ability +50, reinforce-solo +150, buddy +40, battleground +80, non-obj -40). Endor: Bunker first body WITH Part-1 objective relevance but WITHOUT the bonus ≈ 430-470 (BG bonuses + obj +200, no drain/empty-bay/reinforce). +400 → Bunker ≈ 830-870 > 660 so the FIRST body picks Bunker (beats the pile by ~170-240); after control the guard closes and Bunker drops back below Landing Platform, so every LATER body sites normally (drain economy preserved). Additive-domination: outside Endor Operations flipCriticalControlSite is null → +400 never fires (zero cross-deck effect); within it, the intended first-body flip is the fix, not a silent regression, and it self-terminates. Part 1's +200 Endor relevance uses the SAME existing objective-bonus magnitude every other objective gets (V160/V186/ISB) — no new magnitude there.
- Verified: brace/paren balance vs HEAD unchanged on all 4 files; V193 mirrored rando↔chosenone (only the DeckOracle package differs); no `if (false` tape on the edited V136 / parseFlipCondition blocks; new symbols resolve (GameConditions.controls line 3946, DeckOracle.isCardInHand/isCardInReserve both bots). NO mvn run per house rules (orchestrator compiles). PENDING live Endor Operations game — expect log `V193: Endor Operations detected`, isObjectiveRelevantLocation true for Endor: Bunker, `V193 BUNKER CONTROL ... +400` once, a body on Bunker while empty, Establish Secret Base (V) deploying onto Bunker, and the objective flipping.
- Revert: delete the title-keyed Endor block in both ObjectiveAnalyzers (+ the flipCriticalControlSite field/getter/reset/parseGameText lines) and the `V193 BUNKER CONTROL` block in both DeployEvaluators. All new code tagged `V193` — grep the tag to walk every site.

## 2026-07-07 — Six Endor-game fixes (replay qgdridfo166f27r3); all UPDATE/CONSOLIDATE existing rules, no contradictory new versions
### V58/V67w MAINTENANCE FLOOR hardened (mistake 1) — DrawEvaluator both bots
- Rando drew its Force Pile below its maintenance upkeep in the Draw phase → Ap'lek sacrificed at end of turn → Life Force loss. The maintenance reserve was computed (V58/V67w) but only softly enforced (-15), which draw-hunger bonuses dominated. MOD: hard floor near the top of the draw body, BEFORE V42 + all bonuses: if maintenanceObligation>0 && forcePile<=maintenanceObligation && handSize>1 → VERY_BAD_DELTA + early return (Pass wins). Keys ONLY off maintenanceObligation so only "spend-it-and-lose-a-card" Force is hard-protected; empty-hand (handSize<=1) still falls to V42. Hardens V58/V67w IN PLACE, no new tag.
- Boundary: forcePile 1 <= maint 1 & hand 4 → suppress draw, Ap'lek lives. forcePile 2 > 1 → inert. maint 0 → never runs (byte-identical). Revert: remove the maintenance-floor block in both DrawEvaluators.
### V153 THIN RESERVE (mistake 3) — CardSelectionEvaluator both bots, UPDATES V153 in place
- When Rando's RESERVE DECK <= 10 he kept losing Force from reserve, burning destiny draws. MOD: in the V153 zone-order PROTECT-CHARS tier (regular + battle mirror), if isFromReserve && v153ProtectChars && reserveDeckSize<=10 → -335 so the reserve zone lands below hand-characters (100) but above the force pile (50). Survival tier (lifeForce<4) untouched.

### V24.15 EFFECTIVE DRAIN (mistake 4) — CardSelectionEvaluator both bots, CONSOLIDATED (was a separate V189b, folded into V24.15 per feedback_update_old_rule_not_new_version)
- Under opponent Battle Plan (+3 drain-initiation tax) Rando piled bodies onto Endor: Landing Platform (raw drain 1, net 1-3=-2, a drain there is a net loss V189 blocks every time) because deploy-siting only read RAW drain. MOD: extended the existing V24.15 (previously "avoid 0-drain sites") with a second arm — when initCost>0 && rawDrain-initCost <= -2 (the exact V189 predicate), penalize -(300+power*30) cap -700, EXEMPTING objective-relevant sites and V166 contests. Zero-drain arm is byte-identical to the old V24.15.
- Boundary: cost 0 (no tax = most games) → new arm never fires. Battle Plan live: Landing Platform net -2 fires; Cloud City drain-2 net -1 does not; Endor Bunker exempt (objective). Revert: restore the single-arm V24.15 zero-drain block in both bots.
### V193 ENDOR OPERATIONS Bunker plan (mistakes 2/5/6) — ObjectiveAnalyzer + DeployEvaluator both bots
- REFINED same day (Steve): the flip-gate CARD ('establish secret base') was hardcoded in DeployEvaluator — moved into the ObjectiveAnalyzer Endor block as getFlipCriticalControlCard(). DeployEvaluator's V193 is now a fully GENERAL flip-gate steer (reads both the control site AND the required card from the objective logic; log renamed BUNKER CONTROL -> FLIP-GATE CONTROL). All Endor-specific data now lives in the objective logic; the deploy scorer has zero card names. Any future occupation objective that sets a flip-critical site+card gets the steer for free.
- Endor: Bunker sat empty all game, so the cost-0 Establish Secret Base (V, Card207_025 = "Deploy on Bunker if you control that site") sat un-deployable in hand and the objective never flipped. ObjectiveAnalyzer also mis-parsed the objective text (garbage location fragments; whole "...are both" conjunction as one required card). MOD Part 1 (ObjectiveAnalyzer, title-keyed block for "endor operations", scoped — global regex untouched): add "endor" location fragment (Endor sites now objective-relevant), set requiredCardsOnTable = ominous rumors + establish secret base, new getFlipCriticalControlSite() = "endor: bunker". MOD Part 2 (DeployEvaluator V193, NEW tag = new behavior): +400 when analyzer flags the flip-critical control site, Rando doesn't yet control it, and Establish Secret Base is in hand/reserve — steers ONE body into the Bunker (self-limiting: bonus stops once controlled).
- Boundary: V193 is null for every other objective (0 contribution). Bunker 430+400=830 > Landing Platform ~660 for the first body; stops once Bunker controlled. Revert: remove the endor-operations block in ObjectiveAnalyzer + the V193 block in DeployEvaluator, both bots.
